5CCB: BASIC Program
The game is mostly Sinclair BASIC. See the recovered listing on the BASIC listing page. LIST only shows line 1; line 15's header is corrupted on the tape so listing stops. The program still runs. Line 1 does RANDOMIZE USR (VARS-74) digits show 8C, hidden float 74 entering Run_Bootstrap.
